  hydra.core.errors.InferenceError = union{checking:hydra.core.error.checking.CheckingError@{description="A type checking error encountered during inference"}, other:hydra.core.errors.OtherInferenceError@{description="A generic inference error carrying a message and a subterm path.   hydra.core.errors.NotEnoughCasesError = unit@{description="A case statement was missing a case for the requested variant"}
  hydra.core.errors.OtherError = wrap(string)@{description="Any other error"}

  hydra.core.file.FileStatus = record{fileType:hydra.core.file.FileType@{description="st_mode (file type bits) / the type of the file"}, size:int64@{description="st_size / the size of the file in bytes. Well-defined for regular files and symbolic links; implementation-defined for other file types"}, modificationTime:hydra.core.time.Timespec@{description="st_mtim / the time of last data modification"}, accessTime:optional<hydra.core.time.Timespec>@{description="st_atim / the time of last data access, if available. Optional, as this POSIX-specific notion does not map onto all platforms"}}@{description="Metadata about a file, based on the POSIX struct stat. Only the portable, cross-platform subset of struct stat is modeled here; the following Unix-specific fields are omitted for now: st_dev (device ID), st_ino (inode number), st_mode permission bits (only the file type bits are kept, as fileType), st_nlink (hard link count), st_uid (owner user ID), st_gid (owner group ID), st_rdev (device ID for special files), st_blksize (preferred I/O block size), and st_blocks (number of blocks allocated).   hydra.core.graph.Graph = record{boundTerms:map<hydra.core.model.Name, hydra.core.model.Term>@{description="The terms bound by all term variables in scope"}, boundTypes:map<hydra.core.model.Name, hydra.core.model.TypeScheme>@{description="The type schemes of all term variables in scope"}, classConstraints:map<hydra.core.model.Name, hydra.core.model.TypeVariableConstraints>@{description="A mutable map from type variable names to their accumulated class constraints.   hydra.core.graph.Library = record{name:hydra.core.packaging.ModuleName@{description="A common prefix for all primitive function names in the library"}, prefix:string@{description="A preferred namespace prefix for function names in the library"}, primitives:list<hydra.core.graph.Primitive>@{description="The primitives defined in this library"}}@{description="A library of primitive functions"}
  hydra.core.graph.Primitive = record{definition:hydra.core.packaging.PrimitiveDefinition@{description="The host-independent declarative metadata for the primitive: name, description, signature, totality and purity flags, and an optional reference implementation."}, implementation:(hydra.core.graph.Graph → list<hydra.core.model.Term> → either<hydra.core.errors.Error, hydra.core.model.Term>)@{description="A concrete, host-specific implementation of the primitive function: given the current graph, a mapping from a list of argument terms to a result term, or an error. Arguments are reduced and stripped of annotations by the interpreter before the implementation is invoked, so the implementation can pattern-match the argument terms directly; a higher-order primitive whose result shape is fixed by its data argument can return an unreduced applicative term and let the outer reducer fold it (no graph needed). The graph is retained for the higher-order primitives that must evaluate a function argument mid-computation (e.g. lists.filter): evaluating that function resolves primitive names against the graph's primitive table. The legacy `InferenceContext` parameter was vestigial and has been removed (https://github.com/CategoricalData/hydra/issues/446)."}}@{description="A built-in function or constant, consisting of the host-independent PrimitiveDefinition (name, signature, metadata) plus a host-specific implementation."}
  hydra.core.graph.TermCoder = (forall a. record{type:hydra.core.model.Type@{description="The Hydra type of encoded terms"}, encode:(hydra.core.typing.InferenceContext → hydra.core.graph.Graph → hydra.core.model.Term → either<hydra.core.errors.Error, a>)@{description="An encode function from terms to native values"}, decode:(hydra.core.typing.InferenceContext → a → either<hydra.core.errors.Error, hydra.core.model.Term>)@{description="A decode function from native values to terms"}})@{description="A type together with a coder for mapping terms into arguments for primitive functions, and mapping computed results into terms."}
